| `test/difficulty/difficulty.test.js` key-set pin, identity column and layered-merge pin; `test/unit/harness/identityDials.js`; `tools/lib/fit-score.mjs` HELD_DIALS | no `XP_DEPTH_SCALE` | the key, at identity `{ perDepth: 0 }` in both identity columns, an `xpLock` layer `{ perDepth: 0.1 }` in the merge pin, and a held row (user-ruled, never searched) | one new dial; every pre-92.4 pin that runs under the identity override still reads today's flat pay |

`solo-2` (239 actions, dead, depth 2) is byte-identical (measured under both dials). Unchanged on purpose: every shell snapshot (no rendered DOM names a kill's experience), the narrative corpus and ledgers (no game string changed; `node tools/narrative-review.mjs --check` passes), the descend bonus and the table-four XP dots.

New probes (`test/unit/xp-depth-scale.test.js`: the dial and its identity, the multiplier at identity, floors 1, 2, 5, 11 and an elite, the single rounding, a kill and a won parley on a deep floor, the order against the party split and `HERO_SP_SCALE`, no new draw, the descend bonus and the dots unchanged, and the docs that state the rule) are additions, not moves.

### Phase 93 plan 02: the Cloak of Regeneration heals a d6 at once on use (ITEM-08)

Plan 93-02, base `c6d9fc27` (93-01, the view-layer chip order, landed first and touches no engine, content or parity file). Gates at the close: parity glob 66 / 66 after the one fixture below was re-recorded; `git diff --stat c6d9fc27 -- test/parity/prototype-master.js.txt test/unit/roll-high-guard.test.js test/unit/roll-high-save-compat.test.js engine/economy.js` prints nothing.

**The rule.** The user's ruling B of 2026-10-03 ("Cloak of Regeneration should do one tick of healing on activation, then after every x steps."): using the worn cloak heals a d6 at once (in a fight too; a Joiner outside one), then a d6 at 10, 20 and 30 squares walked exactly as before: four ticks, 4 x d6 about 14 hp, up from 10.5. The 30-square window, the 50-square cooldown and the 80-square cycle are unchanged. Data: `act.hot` keeps `ticks: 3` (the WALKING ticks) and gains `onUse: true`; `healTicksTotal(act)` (4) is the "of 4". The instant tick is a `healTick` (no new event type) from `derivedRng(<main cursor>, "healTick", "Cloak of Regeneration", 0, state.steps)`: stream tick key 0, so every walking tick (keys 1..3) rolls exactly what it rolled before. The main rng is only read for its cursor, never drawn, so DRAW_INVENTORY is unchanged. No new serialized field (tick progress is still derived from the timer record's `left`). The fair bot's `knitWindowHeal` reads 4 x 3.5 = 14 (was 10.5) from the item's own activation. The cloak's price stays 1400.

**The predictor.** (a) parity: the chargen seed-4 record declares the Thief's worn cloak with its display text, so the new `txt` moves it (display text only); the encounters fixtures carry a bag cloak whose text `REWORDED_TXT_ITEMS` already strips; no scripted fixture ever uses the cloak. (b) roll-high pins: a pinned bot run moves only if it WEARS the cloak (its `txt` is in the state the hash covers) or USES it (the instant d6 changes hp, and everything after). (c) the save-compat replay replays recorded actions with no cloak use: no move expected. (d) the generated roster block of this file replays parity scenarios that never use the cloak: no move expected. (e) no new serialized field, no new event type and no main-rng draw: the three `*Comparable()` fns, EVENT_NARRATION coverage and DRAW_INVENTORY do not move.

**The live scan (measured with the change).**

1. `node --test "test/parity/**/*.test.js"` on the finished tree before the fixture edit: 63 / 66, the three failures one root cause (chargen seed 4: `worn.cloak.txt` engine vs declared: "engine chargen matches the frozen prototype for every fixture seed", its inner chargen test, and "ENG-05 phase gate"). After re-recording that record: **66 / 66**. `test/parity/prototype-master.js.txt` untouched.
2. `node tools/roll-high-baseline.mjs pins` (runs every label twice, no non-determinism): **2 of 8 moved**, `party-1` (hash only) and `deep-14` (one more action); `solo-1`, `solo-2`, `solo-thief-pilfer`, `solo-magicuser-sorcerer`, `party-fighter-knight` and `deep-8` re-measure **byte-identical** to the pinned table. That is the proof that a seeded run that never uses the cloak plays out identically. `roll-high-baseline.mjs save` was never run; the two labels were pasted by hand with a dated comment each.
3. `party-1` is **text only**: a per-step trace of the run (seed 505, 400 actions) holds no `useItem`, no `itemEffectStarted` and no `healTick`. Proven by two controls: with the OLD `txt` restored and `onUse` on, the run re-hashes to the old pin `de6ebf64...` exactly; with the NEW `txt` and `onUse` off, it hashes to `d3f7b8db...`, the new pin.
4. `deep-14` is **behaviour**: traced step by step (`playRun`'s `onStep`) against a `git archive` of the base. Steps 1 to 9 are identical; at bot step 10 the Thief uses its worn cloak at 69 / 83 hp (14 missing, which covers the new window heal of 14, and 10.5 before, so both bots use it at this step): the base events are `itemUsed, itemEffectStarted` (hp stays 69), the new ones add `healTick` 1 / 4 for 6 (hp 75). That 6 hp changes every later fight and the run dies on the same floor one action later (47 to 48 actions). Control: `onUse` off with the new `txt` plays 47 actions, so the extra action is the tick, not the text. (Control with the old `txt` and `onUse` on: 48 actions, hash `ed08435b...`: the text is part of that hash too.)
5. `node tools/fixture-inventory.mjs --json` in the repo and in the base extract: byte-identical (the generated roster block is not edited); `node --test test/parity/fixture-inventory.test.js` 5 / 5. `node tools/worn-fixture-scan.mjs` (report only): the only record that wears a Cloak of Regeneration on the engine side is chargen seed 4 (moved); chargen seed 2 and the encounters `chest` and `affliction` records list one only on the prototype side or as a bag item (stripped by `REWORDED_TXT_ITEMS`) and did not move.
6. `node --test test/unit/roll-high-state-pins.test.js test/unit/roll-high-save-compat.test.js test/unit/roll-high-guard.test.js`: 24 / 24 after the re-pin; the save-compat replay passes untouched (`expected.hash` did not move).

**Moved.**

| Entry | before | after | rationale |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `test/parity/fixtures/action-script.chargen.json` seed 4, `after.worn.cloak.txt` and its `rationale` (one sentence appended) | `used, a d6 hp back every ten squares you walk, three times; then fifty squares before it will do it again` | `used, a d6 hp back at once, and again every ten squares you walk, three more times; then fifty squares before it will do it again` | display text only, zero draws: the item is never used in the script; the prototype side is unchanged |
| `test/unit/roll-high-state-pins.test.js` label `party-1` (pasted by label; `save` never run) | 400 actions, alive, depth 4, `de6ebf64...` | 400, alive, 4, `d3f7b8db...` | text only (item 3 above): the run never uses the cloak, but its worn `txt` is in the hashed state |
| the same file, label `deep-14` | 47, dead, 14, `9f87e12d...` | 48, dead, 14, `3972ff62...` | behaviour (item 4 above): the bot's cloak use at step 10 now heals 6 hp at once, so the run plays out differently and dies one action later |

Nothing else moved. The unit pins that state the cloak's rule (`heal-over-time`, `item-activation`, `item-text-engine`, `item-wiring`, `honest-gains`, `joiner-item-use`, `bot-balance-close`) were re-based by the edits that made the rule, not by a measurement of a seeded run. New probes (`test/unit/heal-over-time.test.js`: `healTicksTotal`, the instant tick for the hero, with a real rng, at full hp, in a fight, at 0 hp; `test/unit/joiner-item-use.test.js`: a Joiner's instant tick from the member-keyed stream) are additions, not moves.
